SACTCHECK · RELEASE AUDIT

Ten colorectal full suites

v0.83.6 · Eight cetuximab pathways, FOLFOX-4 and irinotecan

534engine scenarios

42browser decisions

20 + 24journeys + service checks

61 / 121PDFs / pages

40QR checks

Software verification completed. Independent consultant, oncology-pharmacy and patient/PPI review remain pending. Source retrieval limitations and unresolved source questions are documented. This is not clinical authorisation.

Decision-map reconciliation

NCCPVersionRulesWhat changed
0020756 → 20
  • Use the canonical indication selector for both colorectal and head/neck contexts; apply extended RAS exclusion to colorectal only.
  • Separate weekly loading/maintenance and recurrence-specific skin restart doses. Preserve the distinct head/neck course: cetuximab begins one week before radiotherapy and continues alongside it.
  • Supplementary EMA infusion, respiratory, eye, allergy, electrolyte and pregnancy prompts are labelled by source.
0073226 → 19
  • Encode the fortnightly 500 mg/m² schedule and 400/300 mg/m² second/third skin-episode restarts.
  • Keep weekly trial evidence distinct from the fortnightly delivery schedule. Current EMA fortnightly approval is separated from historical NCCP licensing footnotes.
00328726 → 54
  • Map weekly cetuximab with fortnightly FOLFIRI and a 46-hour fluorouracil pump.
  • Correct current-count versus nadir actions, current component dose-level reductions, and fluorouracil-only mucositis changes.
  • Expose the ANC two/four-week recovery discrepancy and require complete irinotecan diarrhoea recovery.
  • Merge the severe-renal exclusion and non-quantified fluorouracil adjustment note into one action; the exclusion remains dominant pending source reconciliation.
005855b26 → 56
  • Map fortnightly cetuximab/FOLFIRI separately from the weekly version.
  • Retain source-specific baseline ANC <2, platelets <100 and CrCl <30 exclusions.
  • Expose the ANC recovery interval discrepancy, fluorouracil hepatic equality gaps and fully resolved diarrhoea requirement.
00330616 → 36
  • Keep weekly cetuximab 250 mg/m² maintenance distinct from fortnightly irinotecan.
  • Flag Table 3 cetuximab 400/300 mg/m² restart values as a source conflict against weekly maintenance; do not calculate a cetuximab restart dose from them.
  • Retain the irinotecan renal 50–66% range as a specialist decision, without converting it to a single dose.
00331716 → 36
  • Encode valid fortnightly cetuximab 500→400→300 mg/m² schedule-specific restart values alongside irinotecan 180→150→120 mg/m² table values.
  • Separate current-count holds, nadir recovery actions, severe infusion cessation and unassessed domains.
  • Retain source ranges as review-only decisions.
00692225 → 46
  • Encode weekly cetuximab with FOLFOX-4: fluorouracil 600 mg/m² over 22 hours on each of Days 1 and 2, with Day 2 reassessment/renewal and planned Day 3 completion.
  • The printed administration table lists folinic acid on Day 1 only; require confirmation of the Day 2 order instead of silently adding it.
  • Use the exact source-specific oxaliplatin renal guidance, distinct from FOLFOX-4 monotherapy and previously audited panitumumab combinations.
007332a25 → 48
  • Encode fortnightly cetuximab with modified FOLFOX-6 and a 46-hour pump.
  • Require extended RAS testing despite older KRAS wording in the indication. Label the legacy NCCP diarrhoea Table 6 grading explicitly.
  • Apply the source-specific four-week diarrhoea window, current-level neuropathy reductions and renal guidance.
002109b22 → 33
  • Preserve both adjuvant and metastatic contexts: 12 adjuvant cycles versus duration based on benefit/toxicity in advanced disease.
  • Encode two separate 22-hour fluorouracil infusions and Day 2 renewal rather than a generic 46-hour schedule.
  • Expose the folinic Day 2 question, CrCl exactly-30 gap, fluorouracil hepatic equality gaps and context-specific grade-4 diarrhoea oxaliplatin dose.
00213713 → 19
  • Encode 350 mg/m² irinotecan every 21 days over 90 minutes, without a home fluorouracil pump.
  • Preserve the conditional baseline bilirubin >1.5 to ≤3 ×ULN 200 mg/m² instruction and >3 ×ULN exclusion.
  • Keep 15–20% reduction ranges as specialist decisions; do not borrow irinotecan renal percentages from combination protocols.

Across the full suite

Clinical and patient knowledge, consent, supportive care, date-free cycle graphs, practical access/pump or visit guidance, printable guides/passports/contact cards and source-supported sustainability metadata are connected. FOLFOX-4 retains its separate Day 2 infusion renewal. Weekly cetuximab's head/neck graphic shows treatment before and during radiotherapy. All 24 previously protected patient packages are unchanged.

Validation record · Rule/source map · Browser cases · Service checks · PDF checks · QR checks · Clean overlay verification

{
  "code": "00585",
  "case": "Early infusion overrides mild grade",
  "inputs": {
    "infusion_reaction_grade": 1,
    "reaction_first_15_minutes": true
  },
  "expected": "withhold",
  "actual": "withhold",
  "pass": true,
  "findings": [
    {
      "id": "FIRST_15_MINUTES",
      "source": "page 4.2–4.6",
